On the other hand, it effectively blocks the action of a vomiting centre (chemoreceptor trigger zone - CTZ) located in your brain, which is responsible for inducing the feeling of nausea and vomiting. Pantoprazole helps in reducing stomach acid by blocking the actions of an enzyme (H+/K+ ATPase or gastric proton pump). This proton pump lies in the cells of the stomach wall and is responsible for the release of gastric acid, damaging tissues in the food pipe, stomach, and duodenum."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Does Pan-D Capsule  interfere or affect laboratory test reports?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Yes.           } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="PDP Brand Videos Web"><div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="About Product Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="hn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      in jn
      
      
      ">About Pan-D Capsule </h2><div class="kn "><p class="text-align-justify">Pan-D Capsule  is prescribed to treat symptoms of gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD), acid reflux, and heartburn, especially when these conditions are accompanied by nausea or vomiting. It works by reducing the amount of acid your stomach produces while simultaneously helping food pass more quickly through your digestive system. This dual action prevents acid from flowing upward into your food pipe, giving your body a chance to heal.</p><p class="text-align-justify">To get the best results, you should take Pan-D Capsule  consistently every day, usually once in the morning. It is highly recommended to take this medication on an empty stomach, ideally 30 to 60 minutes before your breakfast. Making certain lifestyle adjustments, such as eating smaller portions and avoiding spicy or greasy foods, will also help this medication work more effectively.</p><p class="text-align-justify">While Pan-D Capsule  is generally well-tolerated, some people may experience mild side effects like a dry mouth, mild headaches, or temporary changes in bowel habits. However, if you experience more serious symptoms such as severe dizziness, muscle spasms, or a fast or irregular heartbeat, you should seek medical attention immediately.</p><p class="text-align-justify">Talk to your doctor before starting Pan-D Capsule  if you have a history of liver damage, kidney issues, or bone-thinning conditions like osteoporosis. Do not take this if you have a known heart rhythm disorder, a prolactin-releasing tumor, or any active bleeding or blockages in your stomach or intestines.</p><p class="text-align-justify">Certain medicines, including specific antifungals and blood thinners, can change how this medication works in your body. Drinking alcohol or smoking while taking this medication is not recommended, as both can irritate your stomach lining and worsen your reflux symptoms. If you are pregnant, breastfeeding, or are an elderly individual, please consult your doctor or pharmacist before using this medicine.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Uses Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="nn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      on pn
      
      
      ">Uses of Pan-D Capsule </h2><div class="qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><p>Your doctor may prescribe Pan-D Capsule  for several digestive conditions, including:</p><ul><li><strong>Gastroesophageal Reflux Disease (GERD): </strong>This medication helps relieve chronic heartburn and sour burps caused by stomach acid backflowing into your food pipe.</li><li><strong>Peptic Ulcer Disease: </strong>It aids in healing painful sores in the stomach or upper intestinal lining by significantly reducing irritating stomach acid.</li><li><strong>Dyspepsia (Indigestion) with Nausea: </strong>It treats indigestion symptoms such as stomach fullness, bloating, and discomfort, especially when accompanied by a feeling of sickness or vomiting.<br>&nbsp;</li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Medicinal Benefits Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="nn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      on pn
      
      
      ">Key Benefits</h2><div class="qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><p>Pan-D Capsule  is designed to help you feel comfortable again so you can get back to your daily routine without digestive distress. Here are the primary benefits of this treatment:</p><ul><li><strong>Fast Acid Relief: </strong>By stopping excess acid production, it quickly cools the painful burning sensation in your chest and throat.</li><li><strong>Reduced Nausea and Bloating: </strong>It encourages your stomach to empty naturally, which prevents the heavy, bloated feeling and stops nausea before it starts.</li><li><strong>Promotes Tissue Healing: </strong>Lowering stomach acid allows the irritated lining of your esophagus and stomach to heal, reducing the risk of future complications.</li><li><strong>Better Sleep and Comfort:</strong> Preventing nighttime acid reflux helps you sleep more comfortably without waking up from sudden heartburn or coughing.<br>&nbsp;</li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""></div></div><div><div class=""></div></div><div><div class=""></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="New Side Effects" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div><div class="dv">Side effects of Pan-D Capsule</div><div class="bx "><div class="cx " about dry mouth symptoms. They may adjust your medication regimen or prescribe additional medications to manage symptoms.</li>        <li style="color: #71716e;">Drink plenty of water throughout the day to help keep your mouth moist and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.</li>        <li style="color: #71716e;">Chew sugar-free gum or candies to increase saliva production and keep your mouth moisturized.</li>        <li style="color: #71716e;">Use saliva substitutes, such as mouthwashes or sprays, only if your doctor advises them to help moisturize your mouth and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.</li>        <li style="color: #71716e;">Avoid consuming smoking, alcohol, spicy or acidic foods, and other irritants that may aggravate dry mouth symptoms.</li>        <li style="color: #71716e;">Schedule regular dental check-ups to keep track of your oral health and handle any dry mouth issues as they arise.</li>    </ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Directions for use Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="nn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      on pn
      
      
      ">Directions for Use</h2><div class="qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><p>To ensure you get the maximum benefit from Pan-D Capsule , please follow these step-by-step instructions carefully:</p><ul><li>Take this medication exactly as prescribed by your healthcare provider. Do not change your dose without consulting them first.</li><li>Swallow the tablet or capsule whole with a full glass of water. Do not crush, chew, or break it, as this can damage the special protective coating.</li><li>Take this medicine on an empty stomach, preferably 30 to 60 minutes before your morning meal.</li><li>If you struggle with swallowing, talk to your doctor or pharmacist about alternative options; do not attempt to open the capsule unless specifically instructed.<br>&nbsp;</li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="How Drug Works Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="hn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      in jn
      
      
      ">How Pan-D Capsule  Works</h2><div class="kn ">Pan-D Capsule  contains Pantoprazole and Domperidone that work together to reduce acid reflux, relieve heartburn, and improve digestive comfort.
Pantoprazole is a proton pump inhibitor (PPI) that works by reducing the amount of acid produced in the stomach. It acts on the acid-producing pumps in the stomach lining and blocks them, helping to prevent excess acid from irritating the oesophagus and stomach. This provides relief from symptoms such as heartburn, acid regurgitation, and indigestion while allowing damaged tissues to heal.
Domperidone is a prokinetic medicine that helps improve the movement of food through the digestive tract. It increases the strength and frequency of stomach contractions, helping food move more efficiently from the stomach into the intestines. This reduces the likelihood of acid and food flowing back into the oesophagus. Domperidone also helps control nausea and vomiting by blocking signals that trigger these symptoms.
Together, these medicines help reduce stomach acid, improve digestion, prevent acid reflux, and provide relief from heartburn, nausea, and vomiting.</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Safety Advice Web"><h2 class="uR sR

      in jn
      
      
      ">What if I have taken an overdose of Pan-D Capsule </h2><div class="kn ">Do not take more than the prescribed dose of Pan-D Capsule  as it may cause an overdose. If you suspect you have taken an overdose, please consult a doctor immediately.</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Diet &amp; Lifestyle Advise Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="nn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      on pn
      
      
      ">Diet &amp; Lifestyle Advise</h2><div class="qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><p>Making small adjustments to your daily habits can greatly enhance the effectiveness of Pan-D Capsule  and accelerate your recovery:</p><ul><li><strong>Eat smaller, frequent meals:</strong> Consuming large portions can stretch your stomach, putting pressure on the valve that keeps acid down. Eating five small meals instead of three large ones can prevent this.</li><li><strong>Avoid trigger foods: </strong>Limit your intake of spicy, highly acidic, fatty, or fried foods. Caffeine, chocolate, garlic, onions, and mint can also trigger reflux.</li><li><strong>Stay upright after eating:</strong> Avoid lying down, bending over, or slouching for at least 2 to 3 hours after a meal to let gravity keep stomach acid in place.</li><li><strong>Elevate your head while sleeping:</strong> Raise the head of your bed by 6 to 9 inches using bed risers or a wedge pillow. Standard pillows that only raise your head can bend your neck and actually increase stomach pressure.</li><li><strong>Wear comfortable clothing: </strong>Avoid tight belts, waistbands, or shapewear that squeeze your abdomen, as this pressure can force acid upward.<br>&nbsp;</li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class=" Carousel-module-scss-module__8NX9pW__sliderContainer  keen-slider__slide   initialCarouselStyle dynamic_section" id="HealthQueries Listing web"><div class="sectionTop"><h2 class="uR sR

However, if it is time for the next dose, skip the missed dose and follow your regular dosing schedule. Do not take a double dose to make up for your missed dose.</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="

      ">Storage</h2><div class="kn ">Store the medication at room temperature, ideally between 15°C and 30°C (59°F to 86°F).   •Keep the tablets or capsules in their original blister pack or container to protect them from direct light and moisture. Do not store them in the bathroom cabinet.</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Drug Warnings Web" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div><div class="nn "><div><h2 class="uR sR
      on pn
      
      
      ">Drug Warnings</h2><div class="qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><p><meta charset="utf-8"></p><ul><li><span><strong>Take on an Empty Stomach:</strong></span><span> Always take this medication on an empty stomach, at least 30 minutes before your first meal, to ensure the active ingredients are absorbed correctly.</span></li><li><span><strong>Swallow Whole:</strong></span><span> Do not crush, split, or chew the medication. Breaking the protective barrier can cause the medicine to release too quickly, which may cause throat irritation or reduce its effectiveness.</span></li><li><span><strong>Long-Term Monitoring:</strong></span><span> If you require treatment with Pan-D Capsule  for longer than three months, your doctor may occasionally check your magnesium and vitamin B12 levels, as long-term use can sometimes lower them.</span></li><li><span><strong>Use Caution When Driving:</strong></span><span> Be cautious when driving or operating machinery if you feel dizzy or experience blurred vision when starting this medication.</span></li><li><span><strong>Do Not Share Your Medication:</strong></span><span> Do not share this medicine with anyone else, even if they have the exact same symptoms of reflux or nausea.</span></li></ul></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="

fetchpriority="low" class="iX wt"></div></div><div class="st"><p><strong>Drug-Drug Interactions</strong></p><p>Inform your doctor if you are taking any of the following medicines, as they may interact.</p><ul><li><strong>Antifungal medications (like ketoconazole or itraconazole): </strong>Pan-D Capsule  reduces stomach acid, which can prevent these antifungals from dissolving and absorbing properly, making them less effective.</li><li><strong>Blood thinners (like warfarin): </strong>Combining these can occasionally alter your blood clotting times, increasing the risk of bruising or bleeding.</li><li><strong>Heart rhythm medications: </strong>Taking this medicine alongside drugs that alter your heart rate can increase the risk of an irregular heartbeat.</li></ul><p><strong>Drug-Food Interactions</strong></p><ul><li><strong>High-fat meals: </strong>Taking this medication with or immediately after heavy, greasy meals can delay its absorption and weaken its ability to control acid.</li><li><strong>Caffeine and Alcohol: </strong>Consuming beverages containing caffeine or alcohol can irritate your stomach lining, naturally increasing acid production and neutralising the benefits of this medication.</li></ul><p><strong>Drug-Disease Interactions</strong></p><p>Inform your doctor if you have a history of the following conditions before taking Pan-D Capsule , as it may worsen your condition or cause complications.</p><ul><li><strong>Severe liver damage: </strong>People with compromised liver function may struggle to clear this medication from their system, increasing the risk of side effects.</li><li><strong>Heart conditions:</strong> Do not take this medicine if you have congestive heart failure, long QT syndrome, or other irregular heart rhythms.</li><li><strong>Gastrointestinal bleeding or blockages: </strong>This medication must not be used if you have a tear, blockage, or active bleeding in your stomach or intestines, as stimulating gut movement can be dangerous in these cases.<br>&nbsp;</li></ul></div></div><div class="fdb "><div class="idb" style="background-color:#fcf2f1"><div class="kdb"><a       in jn
      
      
      ">Therapeutic Class</h2><div class="kn ">[ANTACIDS]</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="Reviews and ratings" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div></div></div></div></div><div><div class=""><section></section><div class="
          } initialBannerStyle dynamic_section " id="PDP FAQ Web"><h2 class="uR sR
      sectionHeading
      
      
      ">FAQs</h2><div><div class="Pn "><div class="Qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq0" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq0" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">What is the use of Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Pan-D Capsule  is used to treat Gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD), Heartburn due to hyperacidity, Inflammation of food pipe (esophagitis), and Peptic ulcer.</div></div></div><div class="Qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq1" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq1" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">How does Pan-D Capsule  work?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Domperidone works by increasing the movement of food through the stomach and the digestive tract more quickly and, in this way, reduces the feeling of bloating, fullness and indigestion. On the other hand, it effectively blocks the action of a vomiting centre (chemoreceptor trigger zone - CTZ) located in your brain, which is responsible for inducing the feeling of nausea and vomiting. Pantoprazole helps in reducing stomach acid by blocking the actions of an enzyme (H+/K+ ATPase or gastric proton pump). This proton pump lies in the cells of the stomach wall and is responsible for the release of gastric acid, damaging tissues in the food pipe, stomach, and duodenum.</div></div></div><div class="Qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq2" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq2" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Does Pan-D Capsule  interfere or affect laboratory test reports?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Yes. Pan-D Capsule  can alter certain medical tests, such as those for neuroendocrine tumours (secretin stimulation test) and urine screening tests for tetrahydrocannabinol (THC). So, before undergoing such diagnostic tests, contact your doctor.</div></div></div><div class="Qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq3" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq3" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">How can I manage my long term side effects?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Prolonged intake of Pan-D Capsule  may weaken your bones and lower your haemoglobin level. Your doctor may prescribe you Vitamin B12 to enhance haemoglobin and calcium/vitamin D/magnesium supplements for bone health.</div></div></div><div class="Qn " role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq4" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq4" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can I take Pantoprazole and Domperidone together?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">A combination of pantoprazole and domperidone is effective and safe.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq5" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq5" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can I take Pan-D Capsule  on my own for acidity?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">No, Pan-D Capsule  is a prescribed drug with minimal side effects. Only take it if your doctor has recommended it to you for acidity.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq6" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq6" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">What is Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Pan-D Capsule  is an antacid medicine containing Domperidone (anti-emetic) and Pantoprazole (proton pump inhibitor). It is used to treat acidity-related conditions.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq7" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq7" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Is it safe to use Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Yes, Pan-D Capsule  is safe for use if taken in the dose and duration prescribed by the doctor.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq8" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq8" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Are there any specific contraindications associated with the use of Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Do not take Pan-D Capsule  if you are allergic to any of its components. It should be used with caution in patients with liver or kidney disease.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq9" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq9" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can the use of Pan-D Capsule  cause diarrhoea?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Pan-D Capsule  may cause diarrhoea as a side effect. Drink enough fluids and eat fibre-rich food if you experience diarrhoea. Consult the doctor if the diarrhoea persists or worsens.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq10" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq10" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">How to store Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Store Pan-D Capsule  at room temperature, in a dry place. Keep out of sight and reach of children.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq11" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq11" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">While taking Pan-D Capsule , which foods should be avoided?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Foods/drinks that aggravate acidity like fried or spicy food, butter, oil, beverages containing caffeine, citrus fruits and alcohol should be avoided.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq12" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq12" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can I stop taking Pan-D Capsule  if I feel better? </h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Do not discontinue Pan-D Capsule  without consulting your doctor. To treat your condition effectively, keep taking Pan-D Capsule  for the prescribed duration.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq13" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq13" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can I take Pan-D Capsule  after eating food?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">It is highly recommended to take Pan-D Capsule  on an empty stomach, about 30 to 60 minutes before your breakfast. Taking it with or after a meal can delay how quickly the medicine starts working and reduce its overall effectiveness. Talk to your doctor or pharmacist if you have trouble scheduling your doses.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq14" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq14" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Is it safe to take Pan-D Capsule  every day?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Yes, Pan-D Capsule  is designed to be taken daily for the exact duration prescribed by your doctor. However, you should not take it continuously for longer than recommended without a medical review, as long-term use requires monitoring of your nutrient levels. Talk to your doctor about your treatment plan.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq15" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq15" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Does Pan-D Capsule  cause dry mouth?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Dry mouth is a recognised, relatively common, but mild side effect of Pan-D Capsule . Sucking on sugar-free lozenges, chewing sugar-free gum, or taking frequent sips of water can help relieve this symptom. If the dryness is severe or persistent, talk to your doctor or pharmacist.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq16" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq16" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can I drink alcohol while taking Pan-D Capsule ?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">It is best to avoid alcohol while taking Pan-D Capsule . Alcohol directly irritates the stomach lining, triggers excess acid production, and can worsen reflux, heartburn, and nausea, thereby countering the positive effects of the medication. Talk to your doctor if you have concerns.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq17" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq17" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">What should I do if Pan-D Capsule  does not relieve my acid reflux?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">If your symptoms do not improve after a few days of consistent use, or if they get worse, do not increase your dose on your own. There may be other underlying factors contributing to your discomfort. Talk to your doctor or pharmacist for a thorough evaluation.</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq18" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq18" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can Pan-D Capsule  be taken during pregnancy?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Pan-D Capsule  is generally not recommended during pregnancy unless your doctor decides that the benefits outweigh any potential risks to the developing baby. If you are pregnant, planning to become pregnant, or breastfeeding, talk to your doctor before starting this medication.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq19" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq19" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">Can Pan-D Capsule  cause bone weakness?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Using medications like Pan-D Capsule  in high doses or for a long period (usually more than a year) may slightly increase the risk of bone thinning or fractures, especially in older adults or those with existing risk factors. Talk to your doctor about whether calcium or vitamin D supplements are right for you.
</div></div></div><div class="Qn ap" role="button" tabindex="0"><input type="checkbox" id="checkboxAccordionFaq20" name="accordionAccordionFaq" checked=""><label for="checkboxAccordionFaq20" class="Rn "><h3 class="vR sR
      Sn
      
      
      ">How quickly does Pan-D Capsule  start working?</h3><span class="Vn "></span></label><div class="Tn" aria-label="Panel Details"><div class="Un " aria-label="Panel Details">Many people begin to feel relief from heartburn and nausea within an hour or two of taking Pan-D Capsule . However, it may take a few days of regular use before you experience the full therapeutic benefits and complete symptom control. Talk to your doctor if you do not notice any improvement.

Capsule 15's\",\"mainEntity\":[{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"What is the use of Pan-D Capsule ?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Pan-D Capsule  is used to treat work?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Domperidone works by increasing the movement of food through the stomach and the digestive tract more quickly and, in this way, reduces the feeling of bloating, fullness and indigestion. On the other hand, it effectively blocks the action of a vomiting centre (chemoreceptor trigger zone - CTZ) located in your brain, which is responsible for inducing the feeling of nausea and vomiting. Pantoprazole helps in reducing stomach acid by blocking the actions of an enzyme (H+/K+ ATPase or gastric proton pump). This proton pump lies in the cells of the stomach wall and is responsible for the release of gastric acid, damaging tissues in the food pipe, stomach, and duodenum.\"}},{\"@type\":\"Question\",\"name\":\"Does Pan-D Capsule  interfere or affect laboratory test reports?\",\"acceptedAnswer\":{\"@type\":\"Answer\",\"text\":\"Yes. medication.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Drug-Disease Interactions\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Inform your complications.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Severe liver damage: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;People with compromised liver function may struggle to clear this medication from their system, increasing the risk of side effects.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Heart conditions:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Do not take this medicine if you have congestive heart failure, long QT syndrome, or other irregular heart rhythms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Gastrointestinal bleeding or these cases.\u0026lt;br\u0026gt;\u0026amp;nbsp;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;53:T6a9,\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Dealing with Medication-Induced Headache:\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;ul style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Hydrate your body: Drink enough water to prevent style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Take rest: lie down in a quiet, dark environment.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Cold or warm compresses can help reduce relievers include acetaminophen and ibuprofen.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Prescription Assistance: Speak with your doctor about more substantial drug alternatives.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Severe Headaches: Seek emergency medical assistance for sudden, severe headaches.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Frequent Headaches: If you get reoccurring headaches, consult your doctor.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Headaches with Symptoms: Seek medical attention if your headaches include fever, disorientation, or weakness.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;54:T4e0,\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Here are the steps to Dry Mouth (xerostomia) caused by medication:\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;ul style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Inform your doctor about dry mouth symptoms. They may adjust your medication regimen or prescribe additional medications to manage symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Drink plenty of water throughout the day to help keep your mouth moist and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Chew sugar-free gum or candies to increase saliva production and keep your mouth moisturized.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Use saliva substitutes, such as mouthwashes or sprays, only if your doctor advises them to help moisturize your mouth and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Avoid consuming smoking, alcohol, spicy or acidic foods, and other irritants that may aggravate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Schedule regular dental check-ups to keep track of your oral health and handle any dry mouth issues as they arise.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;55:T83b,\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Pan-D Capsule  is prescribed to treat symptoms of gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD), acid reflux, and heartburn, especially when these conditions are accompanied by nausea or vomiting. It works by reducing the amount of acid your stomach produces while simultaneously helping food pass more quickly through your digestive system. This dual action prevents acid from flowing upward into your food pipe, giving your body a chance to heal.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;To get the best results, you should take Pan-D Capsule  consistently every day, usually once in the morning. It is highly recommended to take this medication on an empty stomach, ideally 30 to 60 minutes before your breakfast. Making certain lifestyle adjustments, such as eating smaller portions and avoiding spicy or greasy foods, will also help this medication work more effectively.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;While Pan-D Capsule  is generally well-tolerated, some people may experience mild side effects like a dry mouth, mild headaches, or temporary changes in bowel habits. However, if you experience more serious symptoms such as severe dizziness, muscle spasms, or a fast or irregular heartbeat, you should seek medical attention immediately.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Talk to your doctor before starting Pan-D Capsule  if you have a history of liver damage, kidney issues, or bone-thinning conditions like osteoporosis. Do not take this if you have a known heart rhythm disorder, a prolactin-releasing tumor, or any active bleeding or blockages in your stomach or intestines.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Certain medicines, including specific antifungals and blood thinners, can change how this medication works in your body. Drinking alcohol or smoking while taking this medication is not recommended, as both can irritate your stomach lining and worsen your reflux symptoms. If you are pregnant, breastfeeding, or are an elderly individual, please consult your doctor or pharmacist before using this medicine.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;56:Tdc5,\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;meta charset='utf-8'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Take on an Empty Stomach:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Always take this medication on an empty stomach, at least 30 minutes before your first meal, to ensure the active ingredients are absorbed correctly.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Swallow Whole:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Do not crush, split, or chew the medication. Breaking the protective barrier can cause the medicine to release too quickly, which may cause throat irritation or reduce its effectiveness.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Long-Term Monitoring:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; If you require treatment with Pan-D Capsule  for longer than three months, your doctor may occasionally check your magnesium and vitamin B12 levels, as long-term use can sometimes lower them.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Use Caution When Driving:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Be cautious when driving or operating machinery if you feel dizzy or experience blurred vision when starting this medication.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Do Not Share Your Medication:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Do not share this medicine with anyone else, even if they have the exact same symptoms of reflux or nausea.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;57:T518,\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Making small adjustments to your daily habits can greatly enhance the effectiveness of Pan-D Capsule  and accelerate your recovery:\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Eat smaller, frequent meals:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Consuming large portions can stretch your stomach, putting pressure on the valve that keeps acid down. Eating five small meals instead of three large ones can prevent this.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Avoid trigger foods: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Limit your intake of spicy, highly acidic, fatty, or fried foods. Caffeine, chocolate, garlic, onions, and mint can also trigger reflux.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Stay upright after eating:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Avoid lying down, bending over, or slouching for at least 2 to 3 hours after a meal to let gravity keep stomach acid in place.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Elevate your head while sleeping:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Raise the head of your bed by 6 to 9 inches using bed risers or a wedge pillow. Standard pillows that only raise your head can bend your neck and actually increase stomach pressure.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Wear comfortable clothing: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Avoid tight belts, waistbands, or shapewear that squeeze your abdomen, as this pressure can force acid upward.\u0026lt;br\u0026gt;\u0026amp;nbsp;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;58:T83b,\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Pan-D Capsule  is prescribed to treat symptoms of gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD), acid reflux, and heartburn, especially when these conditions are accompanied by nausea or vomiting. It works by reducing the amount of acid your stomach produces while simultaneously helping food pass more quickly through your digestive system. This dual action prevents acid from flowing upward into your food pipe, giving your body a chance to heal.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;To get the best results, you should take Pan-D Capsule  consistently every day, usually once in the morning. It is highly recommended to take this medication on an empty stomach, ideally 30 to 60 minutes before your breakfast. Making certain lifestyle adjustments, such as eating smaller portions and avoiding spicy or greasy foods, will also help this medication work more effectively.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;While Pan-D Capsule  is generally well-tolerated, some people may experience mild side effects like a dry mouth, mild headaches, or temporary changes in bowel habits. However, if you experience more serious symptoms such as severe dizziness, muscle spasms, or a fast or irregular heartbeat, you should seek medical attention immediately.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Talk to your doctor before starting Pan-D Capsule  if you have a history of liver damage, kidney issues, or bone-thinning conditions like osteoporosis. Do not take this if you have a known heart rhythm disorder, a prolactin-releasing tumor, or any active bleeding or blockages in your stomach or intestines.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Certain medicines, including specific antifungals and blood thinners, can change how this medication works in your body. Drinking alcohol or smoking while taking this medication is not recommended, as both can irritate your stomach lining and worsen your reflux symptoms. If you are pregnant, breastfeeding, or are an elderly individual, please consult your doctor or pharmacist before using this medicine.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;59:Tdc5,\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;meta charset='utf-8'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Take on an Empty Stomach:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Always take this medication on an empty stomach, at least 30 minutes before your first meal, to ensure the active ingredients are absorbed correctly.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Swallow Whole:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Do not crush, split, or chew the medication. Breaking the protective barrier can cause the medicine to release too quickly, which may cause throat irritation or reduce its effectiveness.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Long-Term Monitoring:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Be cautious when driving or operating machinery if you feel dizzy or experience blurred vision when starting this medication.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Do Not Share Your Medication:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;span style='background-color:transparent;color:#000000;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:11pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:400;text-decoration:none;vertical-align:baseline;white-space:pre-wrap;'\u0026gt; Do not share this medicine with anyone else, even if they have the exact same symptoms of reflux or nausea.\u0026lt;/span\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;5a:T518,\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Making small adjustments to your daily habits can greatly enhance the effectiveness of Pan-D Capsule  and accelerate your recovery:\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Eat smaller, frequent meals:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Eating five small meals instead of three large ones can prevent this.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Avoid trigger foods: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Limit your intake of spicy, highly acidic, fatty, or fried foods. Caffeine, chocolate, garlic, onions, and mint can also trigger reflux.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Stay upright after eating:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Avoid lying down, bending over, or slouching for at least 2 to 3 hours after a meal to let gravity keep stomach acid in place.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Elevate your head while sleeping:\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt; Raise the head of your bed by 6 to 9 inches using bed risers or a wedge pillow. Standard pillows that only raise your head can bend your neck and actually increase stomach pressure.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Wear comfortable clothing: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Avoid tight belts, waistbands, or shapewear that squeeze your abdomen, as this pressure can force acid upward.\u0026lt;br\u0026gt;\u0026amp;nbsp;\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;5b:T458,Pan-D Capsule  contains Pantoprazole and Domperidone that work together to reduce acid reflux, relieve heartburn, and improve digestive comfort.\r\nPantoprazole is a proton pump inhibitor (PPI) that works by reducing the amount of acid produced in the stomach. It acts on the acid-producing pumps in the stomach lining and blocks them, helping to prevent excess acid from irritating the oesophagus and stomach. This provides relief from symptoms such as heartburn, acid regurgitation, and indigestion while allowing damaged tissues to heal.\r\nDomperidone is a prokinetic medicine that helps improve the movement of food through the digestive tract. It increases the strength and frequency of stomach contractions, helping food move more efficiently from the stomach into the intestines. This reduces the likelihood of acid and food flowing back into the oesophagus. Domperidone also helps control nausea and vomiting by blocking signals that trigger these symptoms.\r\nTogether, these medicines help reduce stomach acid, improve digestion, prevent acid reflux, and provide relief from heartburn, nausea, and vomiting.5c:T83b,\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;Pan-D Capsule  is prescribed to treat symptoms of gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD), acid reflux, and heartburn, especially when these conditions are accompanied by nausea or vomiting. It works by reducing the amount of acid your stomach produces while simultaneously helping food pass more quickly through your digestive system. This dual action prevents acid from flowing upward into your food pipe, giving your body a chance to heal.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p class='text-align-justify'\u0026gt;To get the best results, you should take Pan-D Capsule  consistently every day, usually once in the morning.    \u0026lt;ul style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Hydrate your body: Drink enough water to prevent dehydration and headaches.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Calm Your Mind: Deep breathing and meditation can help you relax and relieve stress.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Rest and Recharge: Sleep for 7-8 hours to reduce headache triggers.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Take rest: lie down in a quiet, dark environment.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Cold or warm compresses can help reduce tension.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Stay Upright: Maintain good posture to keep symptoms from getting worse.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;To treat headaches naturally, try acupuncture or massage therapy.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Over-the-counter pain relievers include acetaminophen and ibuprofen.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Prescription Assistance: Speak with your doctor about more substantial drug alternatives.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Severe Headaches: Seek emergency medical assistance for sudden, severe headaches.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Frequent Headaches: If you get reoccurring headaches, consult your doctor.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Headaches with Symptoms: Seek medical attention if your headaches include fever, disorientation, or weakness.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;5e:T4e0,\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;\u0026lt;div style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Here are the steps to Dry Mouth (xerostomia) caused by medication:\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;ul style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Inform your doctor about dry mouth symptoms. They may adjust your medication regimen or prescribe additional medications to manage symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Drink plenty of water throughout the day to help keep your mouth moist and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Chew sugar-free gum or candies to increase saliva production and keep your mouth moisturized.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Use saliva substitutes, such as mouthwashes or sprays, only if your doctor advises them to help moisturize your mouth and alleviate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Avoid consuming smoking, alcohol, spicy or acidic foods, and other irritants that may aggravate dry mouth symptoms.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;        \u0026lt;li style='color: #71716e;'\u0026gt;Schedule regular dental check-ups to keep track of your oral health and handle any dry mouth issues as they arise.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;    \u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/div\u0026gt;5f:T458,Pan-D Capsule  contains Pantoprazole and Domperidone that work together to reduce acid reflux, relieve heartburn, and improve digestive comfort.\r\nPantoprazole is a proton pump inhibitor (PPI) that works by reducing the amount of acid produced in the stomach. Interactions\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Inform your doctor if you are taking any of the following medicines, as they may interact.\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Antifungal medications (like ketoconazole or itraconazole): \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Pan-D Capsule  reduces stomach acid, which can prevent these antifungals from dissolving and absorbing properly, making them less effective.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Blood thinners (like warfarin): \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Combining these can occasionally alter your blood clotting times, increasing the risk of bruising or bleeding.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Heart rhythm medications: \u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;Taking this medicine alongside drugs that alter your heart rate can increase the risk of an irregular heartbeat.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Drug-Food medication.\u0026lt;/li\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/ul\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;strong\u0026gt;Drug-Disease Interactions\u0026lt;/strong\u0026gt;\u0026lt;/p\u0026gt;\u0026lt;p\u0026gt;Inform your Capsule  work?\",\"field_answer\":\"Domperidone works by increasing the movement of food through the stomach and the digestive tract more quickly and, in this way, reduces the feeling of bloating, fullness and indigestion. On the other hand, it effectively blocks the action of a vomiting centre (chemoreceptor trigger zone - CTZ) located in your brain, which is responsible for inducing the feeling of nausea and vomiting. Pantoprazole helps in reducing stomach acid by blocking the actions of an enzyme (H+/K+ ATPase or gastric proton pump). This proton pump lies in the cells of the stomach wall and is responsible for the release of gastric acid, damaging tissues in the food pipe, stomach, and duodenum.\"},{\"field_question\":\"Does Pan-D Capsule  interfere or affect laboratory test reports?\",\"field_answer\":\"Yes. Pan-D Capsule  can alter certain medical tests, such as those for neuroendocrine tumours (secretin stimulation test) and urine screening tests for tetrahydrocannabinol (THC). On the other hand, it effectively blocks the action of a vomiting centre (chemoreceptor trigger zone - CTZ) located in your brain, which is responsible for inducing the feeling of nausea and vomiting. Pantoprazole helps in reducing stomach acid by blocking the actions of an enzyme (H+/K+ ATPase or gastric proton pump). This proton pump lies in the cells of the stomach wall and is responsible for the release of gastric acid, damaging tissues in the food pipe, stomach, and duodenum.\"},{\"field_question\":\"Does Pan-D Capsule  interfere or affect laboratory test reports?\",\"field_answer\":\"Yes.
